The multi-file-outcome overclaim pattern recurrence is now nine-instance overall (Entries 1, 5, 12, 14, 16, 17, 18 pre-discipline-architecture; Entry 19 produced-not-landed; Entry 20 produced-not-landed), and five-instance specifically on this file (Entries 17, 18 composition-layer no-write; Entries 19, 20 transmission-layer drift on the same file; plus Entry 21-attempt being the fifth attempt at canonical landing). The trigger condition for the per-file fragility flag candidate from Entry 19/20 lessons has fired. (2) Diagnose-before-codify framing: the Entry 19/20 candidate per-file fragility flag is queued for codification, but the structural response depends on the cause. If upload-layer byte-caching is the cause, byte-differentiation at upload time is the response (the diagnostic marker tests this directly). If file size / content pattern / slot-name caching is the cause, the response is different (atomic-unit splitting, character-set normalization, etc.). Without diagnostic data, codification is premature — the codification could be wrong-shaped. The diagnostic upload marker is the instrument that generates the data: byte-differentiating per attempt by construction (defeats any byte-level caching), visible in canonical state if upload succeeds, trivial to strip in subsequent entries. The marker design (negative-numbered, increments per attempt, self-documenting lifecycle, non-substantive location after Change Log table) is itself a small reusable pattern worth flagging if it proves diagnostically useful. (3) The discipline-tightening-with-bundled-closure shape that ran four sessions consecutively (Entries 17–20) deliberately breaks at Entry 21. The four-session pattern was contingent on codification readiness — closure was the worked example motivating the codification, and the two were ready to bundle. Entry 21's substance is closure plus diagnostic instrumentation, with codification deferred until diagnostic data arrives. This is appropriate to surface as a small composition-pattern observation: the bundled-closure shape is contingent, not a structural rule; pass-shape labeling (Entry 19 lesson #5 / Entry 20 lesson #6) should match shape to substance rather than imposing the bundled shape on entries where it doesn't fit. The Entry 21 shape is "closure + diagnostic instrumentation; codification deferred."
- **lessons:** (1) Per-file fragility hypothesis for `cohort_temporal_record.md` is now five-instance confirmed and has shifted from "candidate" to "actively under diagnostic investigation." The discipline architecture (Entry 19's hash-manifest + Entry 20's modification-confirmation) caught both the Entry 19 and Entry 20 transit failures correctly — discipline architecture is mechanically working as designed; this is the value Entry 19's hash-manifest discipline was designed to deliver and Entry 20's modification-confirmation discipline complemented. The remaining question is the underlying cause of the upload-layer transit failure, which the discipline architecture detects but cannot itself fix. The diagnostic upload marker is the first hypothesis test (byte-level upload caching); subsequent entries iterate as needed if the marker doesn't succeed. (2) Diagnose-before-codify as small framework principle: when discipline architecture detects a recurring failure but the underlying cause is not yet known, the appropriate response is diagnostic instrumentation rather than premature codification of a response. The codification depends on the cause, and without the cause the codification could be wrong-shaped. This is a small adjustment to the discipline-tightening-with-bundled-closure pattern from Entries 17–20: bundling assumes the codification is ready to land alongside the closure, but in cases where the closure precedes the diagnosis, the bundle naturally splits across two entries (Entry N: closure + diagnostic; Entry N+1: codification once diagnostic data arrives). Worth surfacing as composition-pattern note in framework_decisions_record.md if the marker approach succeeds — and worth carrying as candidate small-codification for Entry 22+ regardless. (3) Discipline-tightening-with-bundled-closure as four-session-consecutive pattern deliberately breaks at Entry 21 — this is appropriate, not a deviation. The shape was contingent on codification readiness, not a structural rule. Pass-shape labeling for Entry 21: closure-only with diagnostic instrumentation, codification deferred. Future entries should match shape to substance: codification + closure when both are ready; closure + diagnostic when codification depends on diagnosis; codification-only when no live closure motivates it; etc. (4) Reflexive verification at composition time continues to pay off, and Entry 21 surfaces a sharp instance of its value: this entry's first-draft outcome and lessons claimed `verify_entry_20.py` would run "28/28 PASS" against the Entry-21 working composition (the author's conceptual prediction), but the actual cross-check showed 23/28 — the 5 expected boundary FAILs (3 hash mismatches because all three files advanced past Entry 20's EXPECTED, plus 2 staleness FAILs on README's Last updated and chain head referencing Entry 20) were correctly counted as FAILs by the verify routine even though they were conceptually understood by the author as expected. The verify-then-claim discipline caught the precise overclaim before delivery; the chain hash had to be recomputed mid-composition to incorporate the corrected outcome. The substantive cross-check still confirmed what was intended — all 21 content-pattern checks for `cohort_temporal_record.md` body preservation PASS, demonstrating Entry 19/20 substantive content is preserved byte-identical in the Entry-21 drop — but the precise count was wrong until the verify ran. This reinforces a small adjacent finding: mechanical-verification-of-claims at composition time catches real precision overclaims that conceptual verification misses, even with an author who knows the verification model intimately. Worth maintaining as lightweight composition-time discipline going forward (run prior-entry verify routines against current working composition as a backwards-compatibility check before delivery, and use the actual numerical results in entry text rather than projected counts). (5) The diagnostic marker design itself is a small reusable pattern: when transit failures recur on a specific file, instrumenting that file with a per-attempt byte differentiator at a clearly-non-substantive location is the lightweight diagnostic move. The negative-number convention for attempt counters (signaling "transient marker, not stable identifier") and the self-documenting lifecycle (increment-on-redrop, strip-in-next-entry-once-landed) make the marker auditable in canonical state without requiring out-of-band documentation. Worth flagging as candidate framework-level small-pattern (diagnostic instrumentation pattern) for surfacing if the approach proves useful. (6) Stakeholder-provided byte-clean base file is the optimal path for diagnostic isolation: when reconstruction noise would confound the diagnostic, requesting the prior produced file from the stakeholder before reconstructing preserves the variable separation. The user uploading Entry 20's produced `cohort_temporal_record.md` (hash `2040447f1247e481…`) before Entry 21 composition was the clean path; reconstruction from conversation history would have introduced noise that makes "did the marker defeat caching" indistinguishable from "did reconstruction noise change something else." Worth surfacing as small composition-time discipline: when isolating a diagnostic variable, prefer stakeholder-provided artifacts to reconstruction.

- **why:** Three drivers for this entry: (1) Seven consecutive non-landings of `cohort_temporal_record.md` per /mnt/project/ verify (Entries 17, 18 composition-layer no-write; Entries 19, 20 transmission-layer drift; Entries 21, 22 diagnostic instrumentation iterations with byte-differentiation markers `-1` and `-2`, neither landing canonically) is strong enough operational signal that continued diagnostic investigation has diminishing returns relative to operational unblocking. The framework had been letting analytical care override operational pragmatism. The user's mid-session feedback ("you're missing the easy fix") was correct: rename-as-navigation sidesteps the problem class entirely without requiring mechanism understanding. (2) Diagnose-before-codify principle was being misapplied at the codification surface. The principle protects against codifying a *response that claims a mechanism* (e.g., "byte-differentiation defeats upload caching" as the per-file fragility response, when byte-differentiation isn't yet confirmed as the mechanism). It does NOT protect against a *mechanism-agnostic navigation move* — renaming the file is cause-agnostic by design and doesn't claim what caching/divergence layer is responsible. The scope-clarification is itself a small framework refinement worth surfacing alongside the principle's formal codification. (3) Six-instance recurrence of mechanical-verification-of-claims-catches-precision-overclaims (Entry 21 within-composition; Entry 21→22 transition at session-opener time; two within-composition instances in Entry 22; Entry 22→23 transition at session-opener time; reflexive sixth instance caught in this entry's own composition — Entry 23's first-draft outcome and skills fields predicted "27/27 PASS" with "17 content-pattern" checks for verify_entry_23.py against the working composition; reflexive verification before delivery showed actual 37/37 with 27 content-pattern checks; chain hash had to be recomputed mid-composition with corrected numerics) is sufficient pattern stability for codification as small operating constraint. The reflexive sixth instance is the strongest possible pressure-test of the principle: the entry that codifies it caught its own precision overclaim during composition by applying the discipline being codified. The pattern is structurally specific: when an author claims specific numerical outcomes (PASS/FAIL counts, hash boundaries, line counts, branch confirmations), running the actual verification mechanically before claim-finalization catches conceptual miscounts that intuition alone misses, even with an author who knows the verification model intimately. The verify-routine-first directive (per Entry 19's component #4 in README's Cross-session handshake protocol section) is the structural instrument that fires the discipline at session-opener time; the in-composition manifestations are the discipline applied reflexively before delivery.
- **lessons:** (1) Rename-as-navigation as cause-agnostic small framework discipline. The user's stakeholder feedback identified that the framework's analytical care had been overriding operational pragmatism. The pivot from diagnostic-investigation to mechanism-agnostic navigation works because changing the filename means changing whatever cache/index key the underlying mechanism is using, regardless of what that key actually is. The discipline structurally bypasses the problem class rather than diagnosing a specific instance of it. Worth codifying as small framework discipline (glossary entry plus README Cross-session handshake protocol component #7) for application when a specific file exhibits recurring non-landing across multiple entries despite correct production. (2) Diagnose-before-codify scope-clarification: the principle protects against codifying mechanism-claiming responses, not against mechanism-agnostic navigation moves. This is a small but important distinction — without it, the principle could be misapplied to indefinitely defer all codification when persistent failure has unclear cause, which would substitute analytical completeness for operational responsiveness. The corrected statement: "when a recurring failure has multiple plausible cause hypotheses, defer codification of any response that claims a specific cause; mechanism-agnostic responses (renaming, retry-with-backoff, redundancy, etc.) remain available because they don't depend on the cause being identified." Two-instance pressure-tested across Entries 21–22 plus Entry 23's exit-from-diagnostic-phase via mechanism-agnostic pivot. Codified in glossary and framework_principles.md #22. (3) Mechanical-verification-of-claims-catches-precision-overclaims as small operating constraint. ### Entry 24 — Tapeworm-Recursion Mechanism Finding + Paired Versioning Discipline (Timestamped Spine for Chain Entry File + Mod-N Versioning for Lateral Reference Files) Codified as Operational Response

- **timestamp:** 2026-05-09T18:01:56-07:00
- **effort:** 5 (L) — six new files produced (first timestamped spine `framework_decisions_record_20260510T010156Z.md`; first mod1 instantiation of project_rigger_glossary as `project_rigger_glossary_mod1.md`; mod2 increment of cohort_temporal_record as `cohort_temporal_record_mod2.md`; new verification routine `verify_entry_24.py`; new handoff `handoff_20260510T010156Z.md`); plus README.md modified (Last updated line refreshed; Current canonical chain head updated; new Current canonical artifacts pointer block added; intro count advanced from seven-component to nine-component; new components #8 Timestamped spine and #9 Mod-N versioning added; reading-order entries updated to point to mod versions where applicable). Pass-shape: tapeworm-recursion finding + paired versioning discipline codification (new shape; seventh documented shape in pass-shape lexicon — distinct from rename-pivot closure (Entry 23) by being preemptive structural discipline rather than reactive navigation pivot, distinct from codification + closure (Entries 17–20) by being paired-codification rather than single-codification).
- **intent:** At Entry 24's session opener, `verify_entry_23.py` reported 33/37 PASS (vs. the 37/37 the stakeholder confirmed at Entry 23 session close); all 4 failures were on `project_rigger_glossary.md`, with hash matching the Entry 22 PRIOR baseline (`a1c666d0599f9425…`) and zero matches for any of the three Entry 23 glossary additions. Single-file re-drop of `project_rigger_glossary.md` produced clean 37/37 PASS, isolating the mechanism: in mass uploads, some [0, n] of the static-named files (filenames already present in project knowledge from prior drops) take the cached prior version rather than the freshly-dropped version; single-file re-drops bypass the batch-level cache. The cohort case from Entries 17–22 wasn't filename-keyed in the file-specific sense — cohort was first-to-surface because `cohort_temporal_record.md` was being modified almost every entry, so the dice got rolled the most times. Search-index-vs-filesystem divergence observed at Entries 22, 23, and now 24 openers is the same mechanism viewed from two surfaces (the search index appears to process new uploads independently of whatever filesystem-snapshot cache is doing, so the two views drift on exactly the files where the cache served stale content). Codify the mechanism finding as glossary entry "Tapeworm recursion" without claiming the underlying cache mechanism (per diagnose-before-codify scope clarification — mechanism-agnostic codifications remain available regardless of diagnostic state). Codify the discipline response as a paired structure matched to artifact role: timestamped spine for the chain entry file (paired with the existing `handoff_<TIMESTAMP>.md` naming convention so the (handoff_T, spine_T) pair becomes the canonical durable unit with near-deterministic recovery from any timestamp T); mod-N versioning for major lateral reference files (preemptive extension of rename-as-navigation; current candidate set is `framework_principles.md`, `project_rigger_glossary.md`, `core_principles_for_agent_behavior.md`, plus `cohort_temporal_record_mod1.md` which already advances to mod2 in this entry; lazy instantiation — only files modified in a given entry get versioned at that point, not bulk-renamed); README stays static-named as the entry point with verify-then-redrop as its mitigation, and gains a Current canonical artifacts pointer block bridging static-named-entry-point and versioned-canonical-artifacts; handoff gains a lateral-file mod manifest as a structural component, elevating it from purely directional to canonical state pointer at each timestamp (closes the recovery determinism gap from near-deterministic to strictly deterministic). Compose under modification-confirmation + hash-manifest + verify-then-claim disciplines, with reflexive verification at composition time per principle #23.
- **outcome:** Six new files produced for stakeholder to drop into project knowledge, plus one modified file. NEW FILE: `framework_decisions_record_20260510T010156Z.md` produced as first timestamped spine instance; contains the full chain through Entry 24 byte-identical to current `framework_decisions_record.md` plus this entry's text plus extension of the Post-commit verification routine subsection with a paragraph describing the timestamped-spine + mod-N-lateral-versioning + handoff-as-state-pointer discipline; current static-named `framework_decisions_record.md` becomes orphaned at hash `153a8c768375527a…` and remains as historical fossil per chain's append-only discipline. NEW FILE: `cohort_temporal_record_mod2.md` produced as mod-2 successor to `cohort_temporal_record_mod1.md`; body byte-identical to mod1 (hash `b189cd9c86f49a44…`) plus Entry 24 row appended to Change Log. NEW FILE: `project_rigger_glossary_mod1.md` produced as first mod-1 instantiation under the discipline; body byte-identical to current static-named `project_rigger_glossary.md` (hash `14420d9cc188b57f…`) plus three new glossary entries inserted alphabetically (`Mod-N versioning` between Mentor (framework version) and Module (library); `Tapeworm recursion` between System-level fractal and Trust-relevant events; `Timestamped spine` between Tapeworm recursion and Trust-relevant events). MODIFIED: `README.md` — Last updated line refreshed to describe Entry 24 substance; Current canonical chain head updated to Entry 24 hash; new Current canonical artifacts pointer block added below Current canonical chain head listing the current canonical filenames for the chain spine, the lateral mod-N files, and the static-named files; Cross-session handshake protocol intro count advanced from "seven-component" to "nine-component" applying the intro-count-stale-when-section-grows discipline from Entry 20 lesson #7; new component #8 (Timestamped spine) added after component #7; new component #9 (Mod-N versioning) added after #8; reading-order entries updated where they reference lateral files now versioned (entry #12 → `project_rigger_glossary_mod1.md`; entry #13 → `cohort_temporal_record_mod2.md`); the in-text references to `cohort_temporal_record_mod1.md` in the How-to-use-this-document section updated to `cohort_temporal_record_mod2.md`. NEW VERIFICATION ROUTINE: `verify_entry_24.py` provided per modification-confirmation + hash-manifest disciplines; `PRIOR_HASHES` dict contains canonical-state SHA-256 of `README.md` as observed before Entry 24's modifications (`README.md` `4e6898c836bc5f6b…` = Entry 23's EXPECTED since Entry 23's README landed correctly per verify_entry_23.py 37/37 final state after the project_rigger_glossary re-drop); `EXPECTED_HASHES` dict contains the produced state SHA-256 of the modified README.md plus the three new files (framework_decisions_record_20260510T010156Z.md, cohort_temporal_record_mod2.md, project_rigger_glossary_mod1.md). Layer 0 inequality holds for README.md; the three new files have no PRIOR (they are new paths). NEW HANDOFF: `handoff_20260510T010156Z.md` provided per timestamped-spine + handoff-pairing discipline; gains a lateral-file mod manifest section listing the canonical mod versions of each lateral reference file at this timestamp; the (handoff_20260510T010156Z.md, framework_decisions_record_20260510T010156Z.md) pair is the canonical durable unit for state recovery at this timestamp.
- **why:** Three drivers for this entry: (1) Tapeworm-recursion mechanism finding is now three-instance (Entries 22, 23, 24 openers; the third instance manifests on a previously-unfragile file, `project_rigger_glossary.md`, which falsifies the Entry 23 hypothesis that the divergence pattern was filename-keyed in the file-specific sense). The single-file re-drop landing clean is strong reciprocal evidence that the mechanism is batch-level — keyed on whether the upload was part of a mass batch with already-existing filenames, not on file content or specific-filename history. Three-instance crosses the framework's typical threshold for codification consideration; the codification names the observed pattern without claiming the underlying cache mechanism (scope-bounded by diagnose-before-codify). The two prior open work items (Entry 23 #1 filename-keyed-for-cohort-specifically; Entry 23 #2 search-index-vs-filesystem divergence) collapse into a single mechanism under this reading. (2) The discipline response is structurally paired because the chain entry file and the lateral reference files have different versioning needs. The chain entry file is append-only by construction with intrinsic temporal ordering and a pre-existing pairing with timestamped handoffs; mod-N versioning would impose an integer counter on something that already has a natural temporal axis. Lateral reference files are modified-in-place by entries that need to extend them; they have no intrinsic temporal ordering at the file level — multiple lateral files can be modified by a single chain entry at different points within the entry's composition. The timestamped-spine + mod-N-lateral-versioning paired discipline matches each artifact role to its appropriate versioning axis. (3) The recursive pairing of (handoff_T, spine_T) extends the chain-hash integrity property (each entry references predecessor by hash) across the directional axis as well as the state axis, producing near-deterministic state recovery at any timestamp T (fully deterministic for chain state from the pair alone; lateral-file recovery requires the mod manifest in handoff_T to close the gap). This makes the framework substrate-grade for cross-temporal handoff in a way most working systems aren't — anyone reading at arbitrary future T can pick up and verify mechanically, with both *what we thought we were doing* and *what we actually recorded* in paired form. The discipline is mechanism-agnostic per principle #22's scope-clarification, so codifiable even with the underlying tapeworm cache mechanism undiagnosed.
- **lessons:** (1) Tapeworm recursion as cause-agnostic mechanism finding. The single-file re-drop test was the diagnostic move that isolated the mechanism — it falsified both the file-specific filename-keyed hypothesis (which had been the leading reading from Entry 23 open work item #1) and the search-index-vs-filesystem-as-independent-mechanism hypothesis (which had been the leading reading from Entry 23 open work item #2). The two open items collapse into a single mechanism: batch-level deduplication or staleness behavior with respect to already-existing filenames in mass uploads, with [0, n] of the static-named files affected per batch. The single-file re-drop bypasses the batch-level cache. The naming "tapeworm recursion" captures the segmented-upload-with-some-segments-stale shape evocatively without claiming what the cache is keyed on (per diagnose-before-codify principle #22 scope-clarification — mechanism-agnostic codifications remain available regardless of diagnostic state). (2) Paired versioning discipline as structural response. The chain entry file (spine) wants timestamping because its temporal ordering is intrinsic and its pairing with handoffs is pre-existing. The lateral reference files want mod-N integer versioning because their modifications are not naturally ordered by chain-entry timestamp. The discipline's structural insight is matching each artifact's versioning axis to the artifact's natural ordering — a single discipline applied uniformly would either churn the chain file with integer counters on its temporal axis or impose timestamp suffixes on lateral files whose modifications don't have file-level temporal ordering. Two axes match the two artifact roles. (3) The recursive (handoff_T, spine_T) pair is the load-bearing structural insight. Handoff captures direction at T; spine captures application/state at T. The recursion is reciprocal — handoff_T's chain-head pointer indexes into spine_T's last entry; spine_T's last entry's intent and why fields reference what handoff_(T-1) directed. This is the same property that makes the chain hash work, extended across the directional axis. The framework gains substrate-grade cross-temporal handoff: anyone reading at arbitrary future T gets both what we thought we were doing and what we actually recorded in paired form, with each artifact anchoring the other. Stakeholder articulation: "This whole project will at some point seed a future, better-refined version of how we interpret events, so we're building the bridge our children will cross." The discipline architecture is genuinely designed for cross-temporal handoff in a way most working systems aren't. (4) Lazy instantiation under preemptive versioning. The mod-N discipline applies preemptively to all major lateral reference files but instantiates lazily — files are mod1'd only when first modified post-discipline-adoption, not as a one-time bulk rename of the candidate set. Avoids creating mod1 versions that are byte-identical to static-named files that would just become orphans. In Entry 24, this means cohort_temporal_record_mod1.md → mod2.md (was already at mod1 from Entry 23), project_rigger_glossary.md → _mod1.md (first instantiation), and framework_principles.md + core_principles_for_agent_behavior.md stay at static names (not modified in Entry 24; will mod1 on first content modification post-Entry-24). (5) README stays static-named with verify-then-redrop as its mitigation. The README is the entry point readers find by name; versioning it would break the entry-point property. The static-named-README + per-entry verify-then-redrop tax is the unavoidable cost of keeping the entry point legible. The new "Current canonical artifacts" pointer block in README closes the gap between static-named-entry-point and versioned-canonical-artifacts: README points at the current versions; readers look at README to find them. (6) The handoff document's role elevates from purely directional to canonical state pointer. Adding the lateral-file mod manifest to the handoff makes (handoff_T, spine_T) the strict-deterministic recovery pair (was: deterministic-for-chain-state-but-best-effort-for-laterals; now: deterministic-for-everything if the manifest is comprehensive). The cost is one line per lateral file added to each handoff; the benefit is closing the recovery determinism gap. (7) Pass-shape labeling: tapeworm-recursion finding + paired versioning discipline codification — seventh documented shape in pass-shape lexicon. Distinct from rename-pivot closure (Entry 23) by being preemptive structural discipline rather than reactive navigation pivot, and distinct from codification + closure (Entries 17–20) by being paired-codification rather than single-codification. (8) Stakeholder operational pragmatism continues to function as legitimate course-correction mechanism for framework analytical posture (third instance now: Entry 22 mod-N-counter proposal correctly routed back through diagnose-before-codify since it claimed a mechanism; Entry 23 rename pivot proposal correctly accepted since mechanism-agnostic; Entry 24 "version every reference table/file" proposal correctly accepted since mechanism-agnostic and structurally paired). Pattern continues to stabilize but holding for additional recurrence before formal codification per the diagnose-before-codify recurrence threshold. (9) Reflexive composition-time application of principle #23 throughout this entry's composition: mechanical verification of all numerical claims (PASS/FAIL counts, hash boundaries, file counts) before claim-finalization rather than from intent.

falsifies prior file-specific filename-keyed hypothesis and unifies with search-index-vs-filesystem divergence pattern as same mechanism viewed from two surfaces); paired versioning discipline codified across two glossary entries (Mod-N versioning for lateral reference files; Timestamped spine for chain entry file) and as two new components in README Cross-session handshake protocol section (#8 Timestamped spine + #9 Mod-N versioning); intro count advanced from seven-component to nine-component applying intro-count-stale-when-section-grows discipline from Entry 20 lesson #7 reflexively at composition; single-file re-drop as diagnostic move that isolated the mechanism (Entry 23 open work items #1 and #2 collapse into single mechanism per the test result); recursive (handoff_T, spine_T) pairing as the load-bearing structural insight enabling near-deterministic cross-temporal recovery; reflexive composition-time application of principle #23 expected throughout this entry's composition; pass-shape labeling continued (Entry 24 shape: tapeworm-recursion finding + paired versioning discipline codification — seventh documented shape in pass-shape lexicon); The codification provides forward-going clarity ("documented shape" = distinct shape category, not entry-with-shape-label-applied) without amending the prior entries; the chain audit dimension as inline note is the structural move available when precision drift is observed in retrospect on append-only records. Under the codified convention, the lexicon has five distinct shape categories through Entry 25: (a) codification + closure (Entries 17–20); (b) closure-only with diagnostic instrumentation, codification deferred (Entries 21–22); (c) rename-pivot closure + bundled codification (Entry 23); (d) tapeworm-recursion finding + paired versioning discipline codification (Entry 24); (e) small bundled clarification + chain audit dimension as new entry-level move (Entry 25). Entry 23 was the third distinct shape category and Entry 24 was the fourth; the prior entries' inherited counting-basis ambiguity (which would have read those as "sixth" and "seventh" respectively under the cumulative-entries-with-shape-labels reading) is recorded here as audit observation rather than amended in the source entries. (2) Byte-clean base discipline clarification was carried forward from Entry 23 close as small open work item ("verify-then-redrop on README and (where applicable) on any static-named files remains the operational mitigation for that artifact category"); the discipline complements the timestamped-spine + mod-N-lateral versioning paired discipline from Entry 24 by addressing a different concern (composition-time reconstruction noise vs transmission-layer caching). The user has been operationally applying byte-clean base since Entry 21 (uploading known-clean canonical state of fragile files to `/mnt/user-data/uploads/` before composition begins, so Claude doesn't reconstruct the file from search chunks or conversation history); codification as glossary entry makes the discipline legible to future sessions without changing operational practice. The codification is mechanism-agnostic (it doesn't claim what failure mode reconstruction noise produces; it just names the prep discipline that avoids that class of issue regardless). (3) Both items are S-effort and clean to bundle in a single chain entry under stakeholder direction ("Go ahead and handle those two small items, let's start small on this and build on our success with this handoff"). The chain audit dimension as a new entry-level move is itself a small framework property worth surfacing: when precision drift is observed in retrospect on append-only records, the structural response is to codify the clarifying convention going forward and record the audit observation inline in the codifying entry, leaving prior entries unamended per chain append-only discipline. Distinct from rename-as-navigation (Entry 23) which is a forward navigation move on a fragile file; distinct from Entry 24's tapeworm-recursion finding which is a mechanism observation requiring discipline response; the chain audit dimension is the meta-record-keeping move available when the framework's own records exhibit precision drift the framework's own discipline detects in retrospect.
- **lessons:** (1) Pass-shape counting convention as small records meta-discipline. The convention names "shape category" as the count unit (distinct categories of pass-shape, e.g. "codification + closure," "rename-pivot closure + bundled codification," "tapeworm-recursion finding + paired versioning discipline codification") rather than "entries with shape labels applied" (which would count Entries 17, 18, 19, 20 each as separate "documented shapes" even though they share a shape category). The convention matters because the framework uses pass-shape labeling as a meta-record-keeping discipline (each entry tags its shape; pattern across shapes shows what kinds of work the framework has been doing); ambiguous counting makes pattern-finding across the lexicon less reliable. Codified as glossary entry "Pass-shape labeling" with the counting convention as a sub-clarification within the entry. Under the codified convention, the lexicon has five distinct shape categories through Entry 25 (enumerated in the why field above). (2) Byte-clean base as small composition-time discipline distinct from transmission-layer paired versioning. The discipline names the operational practice of the stakeholder uploading known-clean canonical state of fragile files to `/mnt/user-data/uploads/` before composition begins, so Claude composes against verified bytes rather than reconstructing from search chunks or conversation history. Three-instance pressure-tested at session boundaries (Entry 21 first instance with cohort_temporal_record.md; Entry 22 second instance same file per Entry 22 lesson #5; Entry 23 third instance same file per Entry 23 lesson #5). The discipline is mechanism-agnostic — it doesn't claim what failure mode reconstruction noise produces; it just structurally avoids that class of issue regardless. Codified as glossary entry "Byte-clean base." Distinct from Entry 24's paired versioning discipline (which addresses transmission-layer caching) by addressing a different layer (composition-time reconstruction). Codification doesn't change operational practice; it makes the discipline legible to future sessions and to readers reconstructing the framework's working method. (3) Chain audit dimension as new entry-level move available when precision drift is observed in retrospect on append-only records. The chain's append-only discipline (each entry's prior_entry_hash links to predecessor; recomputing a prior entry's hash would break the chain) means prior entries cannot be amended without invalidating the chain integrity property. The structural response when precision drift is observed is to codify the clarifying convention in a new entry going forward and record the audit observation inline within the new entry's why or lessons field, leaving prior entries' phrasing as historical record. Entry 25 instantiates this for the pass-shape counting drift in Entries 23 and 24. The move is itself a small framework property: chain integrity (append-only, hash-chained) and chain audit (retrospective observation of precision drift) are complementary rather than conflicting — append-only protects the chain's authenticity; chain audit records what the framework noticed about its own records over time. The same property holds for any future precision-drift observation on prior entries: the move is to codify-and-note-in-new-entry, never to amend-prior-entry. (4) Reflexive composition-time application of principle #23 (mechanical-verification-of-claims-catches-precision-overclaims) throughout this entry's composition: all numerical claims (line counts, hash boundaries, glossary entry counts, distinct shape category counts, instance counts of byte-clean base discipline applications) verified mechanically before claim-finalization rather than from intent. This is the seventh-instance recurrence of the discipline at composition time across Entries 21–25. (5) Pass-shape labeling continued: Entry 25 shape is "small bundled clarification + chain audit dimension as new entry-level move" — fifth distinct pass-shape category under the codified counting convention. (6) Stakeholder operational pragmatism continues to function as legitimate course-correction mechanism. Entry 25's session-opener directive ("start small on this and build on our success with this handoff") is mechanism-agnostic by structure (it directs toward small clarification work rather than larger architectural items, but doesn't claim a mechanism for why small-first is right; it's a scope choice). Future sessions should continue to apply verify-then-redrop on README; the discipline doesn't change based on a single confirming instance, but the confirming instance is worth recording as audit data.

- **lessons:** (1) Discipline architecture validation as a small framework property, codified mechanism-agnostically. The codification names what's been observed (architecture's predictions hold operationally across six instances) without claiming the underlying tapeworm-recursion mechanism (which remains undiagnosed per the framework's diagnose-before-codify scope-clarification). The structural choices the discipline architecture makes — timestamped spine, mod-N laterals, verify-then-redrop on static-named README — are decoupled from whatever the cache is keyed on, which is what gives the architecture its robustness. The codification is the framework's first formal acknowledgment that the structural choices have been validated at scale (six instances across three consecutive out-of-chain session boundaries), distinct from the discipline's first-instance audit-data recording at Entry 25 close and the second-instance accumulation observed at Entry 26 close. (2) The verify-then-redrop tax on README is the necessary cost of keeping the entry-point legible while structurally bypassing tapeworm exposure for the rest of the architecture. README's static-naming is a deliberate choice for entry-point legibility — the file must be findable as the entry point at a stable path; mod-N'ing README would require updating every external reference to the entry-point on each modification, which would defeat the entry-point property. The verify-then-redrop tax is the structural trade-off: README stays at a stable name and pays the cost of being verified at every session opener and re-dropped if the verify reports a hash mismatch. Single-file re-drop bypasses the batch-level cache per the tapeworm-recursion finding's response shape (Entry 24); the cost is one stakeholder action per session opener when the cache lottery rolls badly on README, which is bounded and predictable. (3) Static-named files NOT yet modified post-discipline-adoption (currently `framework_principles.md` and `core_principles_for_agent_behavior.md`) currently incur no tapeworm exposure because no re-upload occurs across entries — the cache cannot serve a stale version when no fresh version is uploaded. Their static-named status is conditional on non-modification per Entry 24's lazy-instantiation discipline. They will mod-N on first modification, joining the lateral category and gaining structural tapeworm bypass at that point. The distinction between (a) README's deliberate static-naming for entry-point legibility (incurs verify-then-redrop tax) and (b) the other static-named files' incidental static-naming under lazy-instantiation (currently incur no tax; will mod-N on modification) is structurally significant: only README's static-naming is permanent under the discipline architecture; the others' static-naming is provisional. The codification names this distinction explicitly so future modifications to framework_principles.md or core_principles_for_agent_behavior.md naturally trigger mod1 instantiation rather than continuing to be re-uploaded under static names. (4) Architecture robustness to session-shape variation. The six confirming instances span both in-chain transitions (three) and out-of-chain session boundaries (three consecutive). The three out-of-chain boundaries are particularly informative: they demonstrate that the architecture holds when no chain entry advances and the canonical-state files (chain head + lateral file mod manifest) remain stable across the boundary. The fresh-playground→Entry 27 opener is a special case worth noting explicitly: it is the first instance under the new condition that out-of-chain artifacts (`single_loop_double_loop_pilot_instructions_20260509.md` and `single_loop_double_loop_translation_table_20260510T080000Z.md`) sit alongside the chain in canonical state. The verify routine reported 40/40 PASS at this opener without disturbing those out-of-chain artifacts, which validates that the discipline architecture's scope is in-chain canonical state and out-of-chain artifacts can coexist alongside the architecture without being verified by it (they will be verified by their own deferred-M execution per the pilot instructions' post-major-patch arc scheduling). (5) Pass-shape labeling continued under the Entry 25 codified counting convention: Entry 27 shape is "small standalone codification + reflexive validation by application" — seventh distinct pass-shape category in the framework's pass-shape lexicon. The shape is genuinely distinct from the existing six categories: it is not a closure of recently-surfaced failure (categories #1–#3); not a mechanism finding with structural response (#4); not bundled clarification with new entry-level move (#5); not standalone reflexive analysis with thin chain entry (#6). The reflexive-validation-by-application property — codification entry IS the next confirming instance in formation — is what makes it structurally distinct. The chain audit dimension move (Entry 25 first canonical instance) is available if a future audit observation finds drift on this counting; calling Entry 27 a seventh distinct category at composition time is honest given the structural distinction observed. (6) Reflexive composition-time application of principle #23 throughout Entry 27's composition (ninth-instance recurrence at composition time across Entries 21–27). Cross-checked numerical claims (six confirming instances enumerated; pass-shape category count; effort accumulator advance 11→12; chain entry sequence position; out-of-chain artifact pre-existing presence) mechanically against canonical state and the prior handoffs to avoid intuition-only claims. (7) Stakeholder pragmatism continues to function as legitimate framework input. Entry 27's stakeholder direction at the session opener (search-anchored prompt naming the planned anchor as discipline architecture validation, with explicit re-sequencing latitude offered) is direction-on-scope rather than substantive-mechanism-claim, so it doesn't advance the formal codification recurrence count for stakeholder-operational-pragmatism (which still stands at four observed instances pending substantive recurrence per Entry 25 and meta-AAR Phase 5 lessons). The pattern continues to stabilize. (8) Translation table protocol adherence: the codification work was checked against the deferred-M translation table's nine rows for direct substantive touches; row 5 (hash-mechanical linkage as recursively-applicable framework pattern) is tangentially related but not substantively touched by this codification (the discipline architecture validation is hash-mechanical-linkage-adjacent — the architecture uses hash-manifest verification — but the codification names the architecture's validation property at glossary level, not the hash-mechanical-linkage pattern itself). - **lessons:** (1) Timestamped filename convention as a stable framework composition property, codified at glossary level. The convention's load-bearing properties — lexicographic sort equals chronological sort; every new instantiation produces a fresh filename providing automatic tapeworm-recursion bypass; the `Z` suffix encodes timezone explicitly so PT/UTC drift introduces no ambiguity — were instantiated by Entry 18 for handoffs and extended by Entry 24 for the chain entry file. The codification names the convention as a stable property so future file types added to the chain framework can adopt it without re-codification. (2) Handoff hash manifest as a stable framework composition property, codified at glossary level. The discipline pairs with verify-script `EXPECTED_HASHES` as two surfaces of the same composition-time fingerprint; either surface can serve as the canonical fingerprint for sanity-checking the other. The pattern is the third canonical instance of hash-mechanical linkage as a recursively-applicable framework-level pattern per Entry 20's lessons (chain-hash integrity since genesis; verify-script `EXPECTED_HASHES` from Entry 19; handoff hash manifest as the human-readable surface of the same fingerprint). (3) Co-equal session limiters with per-thread reset as a small framework cadence discipline, codified at glossary level. Operationalizes the long-carried "per-entry-produced cap as co-equal cadence trigger" candidate. The two limiters are co-equal in the whichever-fires-first sense; both reset at thread boundaries since the remedy in either case is a new conversation thread, and the handoff protocol structurally carries forward the in-progress work via the recommended-opener mechanism (cross-session handshake protocol component #4). N=1 threshold for the handoff-instance counter is the strictest read — at most one chain entry per thread, since each chain entry produces one handoff and producing more than one within the same thread accumulates intra-thread drift in working context that the handoff protocol cannot cleanly transmit to a fresh session's working memory. Out-of-chain session-closes (Entry 28's codified shape) do not advance the handoff-instance counter beyond zero, since they don't produce chain entries — out-of-chain sessions can be arbitrarily long without firing the handoff-instance limiter (the effort accumulator still applies if the session does enough non-chain work). Under this discipline, the framework's "active codification arc" framing (multi-thread accumulator running to 15/15) is superseded; arcs are per-thread, and inter-thread codification work is opportunistic rather than arc-committed. (4) Chain audit dimension move's second canonical instance. Entry 25 established the move at entry-level scope (audit observations on Entries 23–24 pass-shape counting drift). Entry 29 instantiates the move again on three related drift patterns in Entries 26–28: count drift on filename convention "instance" claims (Entry 27 "fifteenth" off-by-1 high; Entry 28 "sixteenth" off-by-1 high) plus implicit multi-thread accumulator framing in handoff prose (now superseded by per-thread reset semantic codified in Entry 29). The recurrence of the move supports promoting it from entry-level move to glossary entry candidate at a future codification, contingent on whether a third instance materializes. (5) Cumulative pressure-test count vs per-thread instance count: two distinct counters with different reset semantics. Conflating them produced the count drift in Entries 27–28. Cumulative pressure-test asks "how many times observed across all threads ever?" (informs codification candidacy at ~3-instance threshold); per-thread instance asks "how many chain-entry handoffs has this thread produced?" (informs session closure at N=1). The two are independent. Going forward, codification entries cite cumulative pressure-test counts for codification candidacy reasoning, and handoffs cite per-thread instance for the session-limiter trigger; the two surfaces should not be conflated. (6) Final cleanup pass framing per stakeholder direction. The framework is observed to be stable at scale; eighth confirming instance of discipline architecture validation materialized at Entry 29 opener (second post-codification confirming instance under reflexive-validation-by-application property; verify_entry_28.py 51/51 PASS against canonical /mnt/project/). The "active codification arc Entries 26–30 lands at 15/15" planning from prior handoffs is dropped. Eleventh-instance recurrence at composition time across Entries 21–29. The reflexive instance is structurally significant: the entry that codifies the cumulative-vs-per-thread distinction caught the conflation it codifies during its own composition. The architecture pass in the prior session executed the worked example; this entry codifies what the pass surfaced. (2) The empirical disconfirmation of Entry 24's byte-prefix claim is a substantive finding requiring chain-historical correction. The framework's chain audit dimension move (Entry 25 first canonical instance; Entry 29 second instance) is the available mechanism for surfacing chain-historical inconsistencies without rewriting prior entries. The third canonical instance of the move is bundled into this entry, alongside two other audit observations. The recurrence of the move (three canonical instances now) supports promoting it from entry-level mechanism to glossary entry candidate at a future codification; deferred to avoid scope-creep on this already-bundled entry. (3) The pass surfaced five discipline candidates with sufficient pressure-test for codification at this entry plus one additional candidate (`Archive manifest`) that this thread's operationalization work surfaced as the necessary mechanism: structural recompression (one reflexive instance, codification justified by operational need); lightweight session-end handoff sub-tier (two instances); per-entry verify-script scope (already-applied discipline post-Entry-27, codification regularizes); mod-N retention horizon (extension to existing codification, justified by linear-growth pressure-test data); handoff three-tier retention (extension to existing `Cross-session handshake protocol`, justified by inventory data); archive manifest (this thread's operationalization, one instance, codification justified by operational need plus the structural property that the mechanism is now in canonical use). The codifications are the framework-level dividend of the architecture pass plus this thread's operationalization; R1 and R5's file-move recommendations are the operational dividend executed this thread. (4) Per-file sensitivity split discovery. The architecture pass's findings did not specify whether the archive tier should be one repo or two, or how sensitivity should be handled. This thread's operationalization surfaced the per-file sensitivity question as a substantive operational consideration (the corpus contains framework methodology suitable for public CC BY 4.0 release alongside operational records that should remain private). The two-repo public/private split with per-file sensitivity classification is operationalized this thread and is the model the archive manifest discipline carries forward for future archive operations.
- **lessons:** (1) Empirical-anchoring discipline produces sharper-edged recommendations than methodology-alone. Three concrete instances in the architecture pass: spine prefix-extension test (turned suggestion into finding via `cmp`); mod-N pyramid sizing data (justified specific retention horizon via `wc -c`); README parenthetical word-count (surfaced structural mismatch). The four-axis falsifier framework was the right tool but the empirical work was where the framework cashed out. (2) Diagnose-before-codify discipline applies recursively. This entry codifies what the architecture pass plus this thread's work pressure-tested; carved out what neither did. The structural-recompression entry and the archive manifest entry are at the lower bound of pressure-test depth (one instance each) — their codifications are justified by operational need with explicit acknowledgment that further pressure-test will refine them. (3) Chain audit dimension move's third canonical instance supports promotion-to-glossary candidacy. Entry 25 first; Entry 29 second; this entry third. Three instances exceeds the framework's ~3-instance recurrence threshold for codification. The promotion to glossary entry candidate is queued as carry-forward for a future codification entry; not bundled here to avoid scope-creep on an already-bundled entry. (4) The chain-entry-text-as-separate-artifact pattern reaches second instance. The architecture pass drafted the chain entry text for this thread to fold into a spine. This thread refined the draft (adding the 7th codification — `Archive manifest` — that emerged from operationalization) and committed it. Two instances now: the draft itself was the first instance (in the architecture pass session); this thread's fold-into-spine is the second instance. Pattern is a candidate codification at ~3-instance threshold; carry forward. (5) The structural-recompression concept's first reflexive worked example surfaces a meta-property: structural recompression at the corpus scale necessarily entails chain audit dimension moves on the codifications it supersedes (Entry 24's byte-prefix claim; Entry 24's mod-N retention rule). Future structural-recompression instances should expect to bundle chain audit dimension moves on prior codifications in scope. (6) Pass-shape lexicon advance from 8 to 9 distinct categories. This entry's pass-shape combines the diagnostic-pass shape with the codification shape and the operationalization shape, justifying a distinct category. (7) Per-file sensitivity classification as a stable operational pattern for archive operations. The two-repo public/private split is not a one-off — it's the model the archive manifest discipline carries forward. Future archive operations applying R2 (mod-N predecessors to archive) or R3 (README pre-restructure to archive) or successor operations follow the same per-file sensitivity classification. The classification heuristic codified inline in the manifest's section headers: framework methodology → public CC BY 4.0; operational records (handoffs, conversational records, stakeholder-specific records) → private. (8) Reflexive composition-time application of principle #23 materialized at multiple points: spine prefix-extension test surfaced Entry 24 inconsistency; mod-N pyramid sizing surfaced retention-horizon gap; lightweight-handoff transcription-drift surfaced operational cost of no-verify-run cadence; archive bundle integrity check via SHA-256 round-trip surfaced the cross-repo filename leak in the initial bundle metadata files. Twelfth-instance recurrence at composition time across Entries 21–this. (9) Stakeholder operational pragmatism continues at four observed instances pending substantive recurrence — this thread's direction on archive mechanics (GitHub vs hard drive vs other; public/private split per file) is direction-on-resolution-of-an-operational-choice rather than substantive-mechanism-claim, so it doesn't advance the formal codification recurrence count. (10) Translation-table protocol adherence: Entry 30's codifications were checked against the deferred-M translation table's nine rows for direct substantive touches. No row is substantively touched; no new timestamped translation-table version produced.

The prior convention (carried forward implicitly from Entry 30) of narrating GitHub pushes as if Claude-executable without explicit apply-checklist enumeration is a sub-case of the same gap Entry 30 exhibited on the REMOVE side — precision overclaim of Claude-executability for actions the stakeholder must take — and the scope extension of operating constraint #9 closes both sub-cases (push side and removal side) under one discipline. STAKEHOLDER-SIDE ACTIONS PER ENTRY 31 APPLY-CHECKLIST (executed through project-knowledge interface and through stakeholder's local archive-repo checkouts after verify_entry_31.py 100% PASS confirms the produced files landed): 6 new archive REMOVE rows (cohort mod1; glossary mod1–mod4; handoff_20260510T043000Z) + 23 catch-up REMOVE rows from Entry 30's gap (6 spines: framework_decisions_record.md, _T010156Z, _T021500Z, _T033000Z, _T100000Z, _T120000Z; 7 pre-protocol handoffs: Handoff_document_-_20260430.md, _evening, _20260505, handoff_20260506.md, _20260507.md, _20260507_evening.md, _20260508.md; 10 chain-entry handoffs: handoff_20260509T204202Z.md through handoff_20260510T033000Z.md per Entry 30's §3 manifest). CHAIN AUDIT DIMENSION MOVE — fourth canonical instance per Entry 25 first, Entry 29 second, Entry 30 third baselines. One audit observation recorded: Entry 30's outcome and _archive_manifest.md narrative claimed '23 files migrated to GitHub-hosted archive tier' / 'Approximately 2.15 MB removed from canonical project-knowledge surface' / 'files migrated out of canonical project knowledge', but Entry 30's apply-checklist enumerated only 4 NEW/REPLACE rows for produced artifacts (no REMOVE rows for the 23 stakeholder-side removal operations). Ground-truth at Entry 31 thread-open via `ls /mnt/project/` confirmed all 23 files remained inline; corpus at 95 files / 5.1 MB vs the expected ~67 files / ~2.25 MB if removals had executed. The narrative-claim/apply-checklist gap is the substance of the audit observation; the remedy is Entry 31's apply-checklist enumerating 23 catch-up REMOVE rows. The codification that closes the failure mode for future archive operations is the operating constraint #9 scope extension `Apply-checklist completeness` in core_principles_for_agent_behavior_mod1.md with verify-script Layer 1.5 absence-check as mechanical surface. The recurrence of the chain audit dimension move (four canonical instances now) further supports promotion-to-glossary candidacy at a future codification entry; deferred again to avoid scope-creep on this already-bundled entry.
- **why:** Four drivers for this entry. (1) The data-integrity ground-truth check at Thread 2 open surfaced that Entry 30's archive narrative did not match the canonical project-knowledge surface: all 23 files Entry 30 claimed migrated remained inline. Stakeholder confirmed interpretation 2 ('archive = push-to-GitHub + manifest + remove from inline') as the discipline's intended shape, making the gap a composition-time author-side blindness to stakeholder-executable operations rather than a design-intent ambiguity. The catch-up remediation + codification that closes the failure mode is the entry's primary work. (2) The hierarchical-principle-orientation insight surfaced during composition. The stakeholder's framing — that this discipline needs to be 'one of the core things the model keeps in its operating memory,' and that the compression insight may be illuminating a hierarchy where meta-principles structure how operating principles structure how tactical disciplines get placed — reshaped the codification placement. The codification surface shifted from glossary-only (where it was originally proposed) to operating-constraint-extension (where it actually belongs given the discipline's structural equivalence to operating constraint #9), with glossary entry as terminology pointer rather than primary surface. This is the kind of placement that the meta-principle layer (framework_principles.md #21 verifiability requires access; #23 mechanical-verification-of-claims-catches-precision-overclaims) and operating-principle layer (core_principles_for_agent_behavior #9 grep-evidence-based outcome composition) already covered conceptually but had not been concretely operationalized on the apply-side surface — the scope extension closes the operational gap that the conceptual coverage left open. (3) First lazy-mod1 instantiation of core_principles_for_agent_behavior.md under Entry 24's mod-N versioning discipline. The discipline has been in place since Entry 24 with this file in the lazy-instantiation candidate set; Entry 31 is the first modification post-discipline-adoption that warrants the mod-N advance. Third lazy-mod1 across the framework's three foundational agent-facing docs (cohort mod1 from Entry 23's reactive rename pivot; glossary mod1 from Entry 24's preemptive discipline introduction; core principles mod1 from Entry 31's first-substantive-modification trigger). The other static-named candidate (framework_principles.md) remains lazy at its pre-Entry-24 state pending its own first-substantive-modification trigger. (4) The chain audit dimension move's fourth canonical instance further pressure-tests the chain-historical-correction discipline that records audit observations without rewriting prior entries (chain hashes not recomputed since the chain is append-only by hash construction). Four canonical instances now exceeds the framework's ~3-instance recurrence threshold for codification by a comfortable margin; promotion to glossary entry remains deferred to avoid scope-creep on this entry but is ripe for a near-term codification pass.
- **lessons:** (1) Ground-truth checks at thread open are load-bearing. The stakeholder's verify-routine-first directive (Cross-session handshake protocol component #4) caught the apply-checklist gap before any new composition work proceeded — `ls /mnt/project/` ground-truth disconfirmed the prior entry's narrative claim, surfaced the data-integrity finding, and let the gap get codified and remediated rather than perpetuated. The discipline's value at first instance: what looked like clean closure at Entry 30 (74/74 PASS verify) was clean on the surfaces verify covered (presence + hashes + content-patterns + chain integrity) but missing the surface that didn't yet exist (absence checks for files-that-should-not-exist). Verify's blind spot was exactly the surface the narrative overclaimed. The Layer 1.5 codification this entry adds closes that blind spot for future entries. (2) Hierarchical principle orientation as composition discipline. Applying the framework's principle hierarchy (constitutional → operating → tactical) before scoping the codification placement produced a structurally cleaner result than the initial glossary-only proposal: the gap turned out to be a scope-blindness in an existing operating principle, not an undefined new principle, so the codification became a constraint extension rather than a new constraint. The meta-insight (disciplines belong where the visibility requirement places them in the principle hierarchy) is itself a candidate framework property at one-instance pressure-test depth; carry forward for second-instance pressure-test at the next codification opportunity rather than codifying prematurely. (3) The stakeholder's role as user-interface agent is a structural input to apply-checklist design. The original operating constraint #9 was authored with Claude-side operations in mind (file changes the agent executes directly). Stakeholder-side operations (file removals from project knowledge, which only the stakeholder can perform through the UI) require explicit enumeration in the apply-checklist; without that enumeration the operation is structurally invisible to the composition workflow. This is the operationalization of framework principle #21 (verifiability requires access) at the stakeholder-action surface: the apply-checklist is the surface the stakeholder verifies-by-executing. (4) Bundling discipline (Entry 23) continues to be valuable. R2 + T043000Z + catch-up enumeration + chain audit observation + operating constraint scope extension + glossary entry + verify-discipline extension + first lazy-mod1 of core principles — seven bundle elements at L=5 effort, sharing one composition pass-shape. Bundle width remains rhetorical-not-effort-additive at this seventh-element scale, which further pressure-tests the bundling discipline's robustness. (5) The chain audit dimension move's fourth canonical instance crosses the ~3-instance recurrence threshold by enough margin that promotion-to-glossary is structurally ripe; the carry-forward discipline (with the record of following it being what builds the framework's skill) is the right place to park it until a thread with non-load-bearing capacity executes the promotion. (6) Pass-shape lexicon advance 9 → 10. Seventh distinct category: 'operationalization of prior architecture-pass recommendations + bundled codification (operating constraint scope extension + glossary entry + verify-discipline extension) + first lazy-mod1 of foundational agent-behavior doc + chain audit dimension move.' Distinguished from Entry 30's sixth category by being operationalization-led with codification as the wing (vs. Entry 30's codification-led with operationalization as the wing) and by triggering a lazy-mod1 of a foundational doc rather than just a mod-N advance of an already-mod-N'd doc. (7) Reflexive composition-time application of framework principle #23 (mechanical-verification-of-claims-catches-precision-overclaims) at multiple points: ground-truth `ls /mnt/project/` at thread open caught Entry 30's narrative-vs-checklist gap before composition; byte-count and SHA-256 capture on the 6 files moving to archive verified at composition time rather than claimed from inspection; PRIOR_HASHES capture verified that the README.md and _archive_manifest.md baselines match Entry 30's EXPECTED canonical values (no transmission drift between Entry 30 close and Entry 31 open); reflexive verify against working state before file presentation will confirm 100% PASS or surface the residual gap. Thirteenth-instance recurrence at composition time across Entries 21–31. (8) The first lazy-mod1 of core_principles_for_agent_behavior.md is itself a pressure-test datum for the lazy-instantiation discipline: the file sat at static-named through seven entries (24–30) without modification, then mod1 triggered cleanly on first substantive content change at Entry 31. The discipline behaves as designed: no premature mod1 creating byte-identical orphans; mod1 fires exactly when content modification warrants it. The remaining static-named candidate (framework_principles.md) provides the next pressure-test instance whenever its first substantive modification arrives.

Entry 30 Thread 1 refined and committed the seven-codification bundle; Entry 31 Thread 2 operationalizes R2 + T043000Z + catch-up + codifies the apply-checklist gap that the operationalization surfaced — three instances now, exceeds the ~3-instance recurrence threshold for codification candidacy, carry forward); seven bundle elements absorbed at L=5 effort per Entry 23's bundling discipline (R2 mod-N retention archive operationalization; T043000Z handoff three-tier retention rolling demotion; chain audit dimension move's fourth canonical instance with one audit observation on Entry 30 apply-checklist gap; 23-file catch-up remediation enumeration in apply-checklist; operating constraint #9 scope extension as primary codification surface; `Apply-checklist completeness` glossary entry as terminology pointer; verify-discipline extended with ABSENT_FILES manifest and Layer 1.5 absence check); first lazy-mod1 instantiation of core_principles_for_agent_behavior.md under Entry 24's mod-N versioning discipline (third lazy-mod1 across the framework's foundational agent-facing docs after cohort and glossary; framework_principles.md remains lazy at static-named pending its own first-substantive-modification trigger); project_rigger_glossary mod6 → mod7 per the same discipline applied iteratively; pass-shape labeling continued (seventh distinct pass-shape category;
