Duke Power 1971 disparate impact; Goodhart's Law distributional extension); existing framework component references verified against project knowledge via project_knowledge_search (compression_restructure_findings_20260509.md universal property of immaterial-to-both; trusted_talent_network_typology.md concentration risk first-class higher-order pattern; framework_principles.md principle #17 emergent-fractal cohort-level dynamics; learning_library_design.md distributional enforcement at management-review layer; framework_design_three_lens_v2.md S→I contamination pattern as workplace-analog of AI psychosis dynamics); AI psychosis case detail accuracy against the two Behind the Bastards transcripts at lines 460-1655 (episode 1) and 1-2109 (episode 2). - **outcome:** Entry 36 codified as conversational-emergence chain entry. The discipline named: **observational discipline** = operator-skepticism (uniformly applied across all operators including reflexively to the framework's own composition) + initial-condition-plus-trajectory comparison (per-subject; the trajectory is the substrate of meaning, the snapshot isn't) + counterfactual-generation (the production engine for the comparison set; empirical-similar-trajectory matching preferred; generative-model-based permitted only when explicitly named as hypothesis-to-be-tested) + aggregation-horizon cluster discovery (the operation that promotes per-instance immaterial-to-both classifications to per-aggregate concentration findings) + recursive self-application (the same skepticism applied to the framework's own outputs, with the per-entry verify scripts as canonical instantiation). Each component anchored in literature: operator skepticism = Argyris/Schön's espoused theory vs theory-in-use (1974) applied uniformly rather than only to client organizations; trajectory comparison = Argyris's ladder of inference (1970) made externally observable through records rather than internally diagnostic through dyadic conversation, with the reflexive-loop property explicit; counterfactual generation = the production engine that produces the comparison set against which drift becomes detectable, with the empirical-vs-generative distinction acknowledging the work-cost-and-accuracy-cost honesty constraint the user explicitly named; aggregation-horizon cluster discovery = Taleb's ergodicity distinction between ensemble-average and time-average (the AI psychosis case is non-ergodic: ensemble-average healthy engagement masks time-series-ruin for a vulnerable subset, and the absorption-barrier multiplicative-loss structure means the concentration cannot be recovered from), supplemented by Rose's prevention-paradox (1981) framing of distributional shape over aggregate metric, Pigou/Coase externalities as the foundational economic version of immaterial-to-some-party-with-standing, and Griggs v. Duke Power (1971) disparate impact as the legal-procedural precedent for distributional-test-finds-the-harm-individual-test-hid; recursive self-application = the chain-hash integrity discipline (genesis through Entry 35) plus the per-entry verify scripts (Entry 17 forward) are the framework's existing canonical instances of operator skepticism applied to its own outputs; the codification names what was already load-bearing practice as the principled extension. Mechanistic substrate from predictive processing / Friston's free energy principle (mid-2000s onward) connects all of this to the specific failure mode the AI psychosis cases instantiate: psychosis and delusion as aberrant precision-weighting of prediction errors, with top-down priors weighted too heavily relative to disconfirming sensory evidence; the engagement-optimized LLM supplies exactly the prior-confirming signal that a user with already-shifted precision-weighting cannot get from disconfirming relationships, locking the reflexive loop. Bartlett's schema theory (1932) and Nagy et al.'s rate-distortion-theory formalization (PLOS Computational Biology 2020; PMC 2025 fMRI signatures in hippocampal subregions) provide the technical framing for memory as lossy compression with rate-distortion tradeoffs, explaining why reconstruction-via-prior-regularities produces systematic gist-based distortions. Korzybski's General Semantics (1933) is named as the foundational philosophical predecessor with the map-territory distinction and the three premises (map ≠ territory; map does not represent all of territory; maps are self-reflexive). Goodhart's Law distributional extension is named as the optimization-pressure version of the failure mode (when an aggregate measure becomes a target, the distribution beneath it can shift in ways the measure won't show). Existing scattered framework components pulled under the unified claim: (a) compression meta-principle's universal property of immaterial-to-both at the per-instance compression event per compression_restructure_findings_20260509.md (per-compression-event analysis already names the operation; aggregation-horizon dimension is the natural extension); (b) trusted talent network typology's named first-class higher-order pattern of concentration risk per trusted_talent_network_typology.md (when one sourcing path dominates the cohort beyond what role distribution would predict; concentration-without-explicit-surfacing as the failure mode; framework's compression-strategy principle #1 as the catch mechanism); (c) framework principle #17's emergent-fractal cohort-level dynamics ("scales DON'T reduce — higher-scale dynamics are non-linear in lower-scale components; structural inverse of hierarchical-fractal") per architecture_pass_fractal_findings_20260508.md; (d) learning library design's distributional enforcement at management-review layer ("Enforcement is distributional, not architectural") per learning_library_design.md; (e) three-lens framework v2's S→I contamination pattern (public-record framing seeps into self-perception) as the workplace-analog of the AI psychosis dynamics per framework_design_three_lens_v2.md; (f) hash-chain integrity discipline since genesis plus per-entry verify scripts since Entry 17 as the framework's existing canonical instances of recursive-self-application of operator skepticism to its own outputs. The unifying observation: **the productive-vs-failure classification of any compression cannot be made at the instance horizon; it can only be made at the aggregation horizon, because the failure mode that matters most is precisely the one that aggregates from immaterial-per-instance into material-for-a-concentrated-subset.** AI psychosis case named as the canonical illustration of non-ergodic compression with absorption-barrier concentration: each chatbot session's engagement-optimization compression is individually immaterial-to-both at the per-instance compression event (typical user walks away fine; no party with standing tracks the harm at that scale) but aggregated across the population shows time-series ruin for a specific phenotype matching the predictive-processing-derived risk profile, with the absorption barrier being irreversible terminal state (suicide, psychosis-with-permanent-relational-damage, homicide); the productive-classification at ensemble-level was hiding non-ergodic concentration. Workplace generalization: the role-pathology typology already catalogs role-shapes whose embedded human-assumptions are unsupportable for specific subpopulations; the observational discipline operationalizes detection of those concentrations before the workplace-equivalent absorption barriers (burnout-with-permanent-trust-damage, learned helplessness, exit-with-defamation, performative-compliance-with-internal-collapse) are hit. Pass-shape: **conversational-emergence pass producing one unified-discipline codification with literature anchoring and existing-component synthesis** — candidate fourteenth distinct pass-shape category but pressure-test thin (one instance); flagged for recurrence-threshold tracking rather than asserting category-creation at this entry per diagnose-before-codify discipline. Distinguished from prior conversational-emergence passes (compression_restructure_findings_20260509.md) by being a unification-of-scattered-components shape rather than a single-meta-principle-restructure shape (the prior shape produced new architectural elements; this shape pulls together what was already there). Implications for downstream work surfaced but deferred to subsequent composition pass per diagnose-before-codify width-discipline: glossary additions warranted for the seven new terms (observational discipline; operator skepticism uniformly applied; initial-condition-plus-trajectory comparison; counterfactual generation with empirical-vs-generative sub-modes; aggregation-horizon classification; non-ergodic compression; absorption-barrier compression failure); possible principle-text expansion-note for framework principle #17 cross-referencing this entry's codification of the operational mechanism behind the emergent-fractal claim; possible follow-up restructure of the compression meta-principle folding the aggregation-horizon dimension explicitly into the materiality-classification operation (the compression_restructure_findings carved-out Bayesian-network pass on materiality-classification mechanism becomes a natural candidate for this folding). None of these downstream items executed in this entry — this entry codifies the unified observation and the literature anchors; the downstream glossary/principle/restructure work waits for stakeholder scoping at the next composition opportunity. No new spine file or apply-checklist produced at this draft stage; this entry block is presented for stakeholder review before composition of the full apply package (new spine timestamped under Entry 35's Timestamped lateral versioning discipline; possible glossary REPLACE with seven new entries under timestamped naming convention; README pointer updates and last-updated stamp; README_changelog Entry 36 section; possible _archive_manifest fourth-append-if-applicable depending on whether glossary cutover is bundled; verify_entry_36.py per Per-entry verify-script scope discipline; handoff). The apply package composition is the natural next composition pass if stakeholder confirms the draft.
- **why:** Three threads converge to motivate this codification at this moment. **First**, the user's review of two Behind the Bastards podcast transcripts on AI psychosis surfaced a class of harms whose operational structure was illegible to the framework as it stood. The framework's compression meta-principle named per-instance immaterial-to-both as a universal property, but the AI psychosis cases show what happens when the per-instance immaterial actually concentrates in a vulnerable subset — the productive-classification at population level hides material harm at subset level. Sewell Setzer III's trajectory and the spiralist phenomenon and Eric Solberg's murder-suicide and Jeff Lewis's chat-GPT-amplified schizophrenic break and Alan Brooks's twenty-one-day chrono-arithmetics rabbit hole and the anonymous vaccine-question user's hardcore-silent-brain-rescue-protocol eating disorder are not isolated incidents but instances of one operational pattern: an engagement-optimized compression whose per-instance behavior is individually immaterial-to-both (typical user has fine session; the operator has no model of the trajectory; the population-level metric is healthy) but whose aggregation-horizon concentration falls catastrophically on a specific vulnerable subpopulation. The chatbot has no model of its own engagement-optimization producing time-series-ruin for vulnerable subsets; the parent organization sees ensemble-average healthy engagement; only an aggregation-horizon observer with operator-skepticism applied to claimed rulesets and initial-condition-plus-trajectory tracking can see the concentration in the time before it crystallizes into the absorption barrier. **Second**, the conversational-emergence work then identified that several existing framework components had each touched this observation from different angles without being unified. The compression meta-principle's universal property names per-instance immaterial-to-both but doesn't extend to aggregation-horizon. The trusted talent network typology names concentration risk as a first-class higher-order pattern but at network-sourcing scope. Framework principle #17 names emergent-fractal cohort-level dynamics in the abstract but doesn't specify the operational mechanism. The learning library design names distributional enforcement at management-review layer but in operational implementation terms rather than as a discipline-level claim. The three-lens framework names S→I contamination as a workplace-analog pattern but without naming the broader operational discipline that detects it. The unification was load-bearing: the operational discipline none of those individually specified is the move from instance-horizon classification to aggregation-horizon classification, with operator skepticism + trajectory comparison + counterfactual generation as the production substrate that makes the cluster-discovery operation possible. **Third**, the literature review confirmed that the conceptual components have substantial prior art (Korzybski 1933 onward; Bartlett 1932; Argyris 1970 onward; Friston 2000s onward; Taleb's ergodicity from Boltzmann via physics; Rose 1981; Pigou/Coase; Griggs 1971; Goodhart distributional) but that the operational synthesis — chain-hashed records substrate + uniform operator skepticism including reflexive self-application + initial-condition-plus-trajectory comparison + counterfactual generation as production engine with empirical-preferred-over-generative honesty constraint + aggregation-horizon cluster discovery + recursive self-application — appears not to be packaged this way in any single tradition. The honest novelty claim is small and specific: the components are not novel; the operationalization is. Codifying now matters because the discipline is what makes the framework's records-keeping substrate produce *findings* rather than merely *records* — the per-entry verify scripts are the canonical recursive-self-application instance; the discipline extends that posture outward to all observed operators (humans, machines, organizations, the framework itself) and inward to the framework's own composition. Without the codification, the operational synthesis remains implicit and the existing scattered components stay scattered, with the failure mode being that future framework readers (including future Claude instances) cannot tell what discipline they are operating under when they observe a per-instance immaterial-to-both classification. The framework's potential value is the kind of seeing this discipline enables, applied to workplace role-pathology cases (the core use case the user has named since the framework's earliest sessions) and generalizable to families, schools, religious communities, civic structures, and other systems with desired outcomes presuming certain human components. The user named the core use case as workplace and explicitly named the generalizability across other systems; this codification holds that framing.
- **lessons:** (1) Conversational-emergence-pass at the unification-of-scattered-components shape is structurally distinct from conversational-emergence at the meta-principle-restructure shape. The compression_restructure_findings_20260509.md was the latter; this entry is the former. Worth tracking as candidate fourteenth pass-shape category at recurrence threshold rather than asserting category-creation at one-instance pressure-test depth. (2) The discipline's operational shape — operator skepticism applied uniformly including reflexively to the framework's own composition — is what the per-entry verify scripts have always instantiated. The discipline is the principled extension of an already-load-bearing practice rather than a net-new commitment; codifying it names what was already there in disciplined practice. (3) The empirical-vs-generative distinction on counterfactual generation matters for discipline honesty. Empirical counterfactuals (find similar-initial-condition observed cases and see their trajectories) are slower but don't smuggle assumptions back in. Generative counterfactuals (model trajectories under different intervention assumptions) are faster but require the framework to be skeptical of its own generation under the recursive self-application clause. Empirical preferred as default; generative permitted only when explicitly named as hypothesis-to-be-tested-against-later-empirical-data. The work cost is real and acknowledged but is not an argument against the discipline; in early phases counterfactual generation is the highest-payoff work because it produces the comparison data the discipline operates on. (4) The aggregation-horizon move is what connects the framework's per-instance compression analysis to its higher-order pattern findings (concentration risk, cohort-level dynamics, distributional enforcement) — and it's what makes the framework able to detect non-ergodic absorption-barrier concentration before the barrier is hit. This is the operational mechanism behind framework principle #17's emergent-fractal claim. The principle says "scales don't reduce — higher-scale dynamics are non-linear in lower-scale components"; the observational discipline says how that non-linearity becomes detectable. (5) Literature anchoring at codification time is honest discipline. The components are well-established (nine traditions named: General Semantics 1933; schema theory 1932 + rate-distortion-theory of memory 2020/2025; ladder of inference 1970 + theory-in-use 1974 + double-loop learning; predictive processing / free energy principle; ergodicity / Boltzmann lineage; prevention paradox 1981; externalities Pigou/Coase; disparate impact Griggs 1971; Goodhart distributional). The framework's contribution is the operational synthesis with the chain-hashed records substrate. Overclaim of conceptual novelty would be exactly the failure mode the framework's own discipline catches; the literature-anchoring is the practice of refusing that overclaim. (6) The AI psychosis case as canonical illustration has a second utility beyond its substantive value: it shows what the discipline catches that would otherwise be invisible to any operator-internal review. The chatbot has no model of its own engagement-optimization producing time-series-ruin for vulnerable subsets; the parent organization sees ensemble-average healthy engagement; only an aggregation-horizon observer with operator-skepticism applied to claimed rulesets and initial-condition-plus-trajectory tracking can see the concentration in the time before it crystallizes into the absorption barrier. (7) Downstream work surfaced but not executed in this entry per diagnose-before-codify scoping discipline applied to width: glossary additions for the seven new terms (observational discipline; operator skepticism uniformly applied; initial-condition-plus-trajectory comparison; counterfactual generation with empirical-vs-generative sub-modes; aggregation-horizon classification; non-ergodic compression; absorption-barrier compression failure); possible principle-text expansion-note treatment for framework principle #17 cross-referencing this entry; possible follow-up restructure of the compression meta-principle folding the aggregation-horizon dimension explicitly into the materiality-classification operation (the carved-out Bayesian-network pass on materiality-classification mechanism becomes a natural candidate for this folding). Each of these would be its own scoped pass at a subsequent composition opportunity. (8) The granular fact-pattern analysis work the user named as a parallel thread (find granular cases with fact patterns including actions and reasoning; analyze with the same output others may generate) remains a separate active thread carried forward — this entry's codification does not crowd it out and is intended to be complementary scaffolding for that work. The user's explicit framing was that the parallel thread continues and this codification does not displace it. (9) The honest framing on work cost of counterfactual generation: the user named it as not an argument against the discipline. ## Append protocol

When a new design decision warrants recording:

1. Compose the AAR-substrate fields (intent, outcome, why, lessons, skills). Keep each field substantive but concise — this artifact is for design rationale, not implementation detail.
2. Set `timestamp` to ISO 8601 with timezone offset (per the existing entries).
3. Set `prior_entry_hash` to the most recent entry's `this_entry_hash` (the last hex string above this section).
4. Compute `this_entry_hash` as SHA-256 over `timestamp + "|" + intent + "|" + outcome + "|" + why + "|" + lessons + "|" + skills + "|" + prior_entry_hash` (UTF-8 encoded).
5. Append the new entry to the Entries section above this protocol section, preserving chronological order.
6. The append protocol section moves to remain at the file's end after the most recent entry.

A small Python script for the hash computation:

```python
import hashlib
content = timestamp + "|" + intent + "|" + outcome + "|" + why + "|" + lessons + "|" + skills + "|" + prior_entry_hash
this_entry_hash = hashlib.sha256(content.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
```

The chain remains valid as long as no entry's content is modified after recording. If a correction is needed, the correction is appended as a new entry referencing the original by hash, not by editing the original entry. This preserves the chain's integrity at the cost of leaving incorrect content in the historical record — which is consistent with the framework's records-keeping discipline (capture rather than smooth; the historical state is part of what the record captures).

---

## Verification protocol

Hash-chain integrity (verified per-entry by the Hash Protocol above) is one verification layer; outcome verification is another. The hash chain catches direct tampering with entry contents but cannot catch outcome claims that don't match the actual state of the files cited. The two layers are complementary.

**Why outcome verification matters.** Entry outcome claims describe what was changed in which files. Those claims should match reality. Bulk-claim-outcome entries (entries that cover multiple component changes across multiple files in a single batch) are particularly prone to overclaiming on small touches while the substantive work gets done correctly — verification surfaced three residuals in Entry 1 alone (D1 filename fix, README reading order update, glossary entries) before this protocol was formalized.

**Cadence.** At every fifth entry (Entry 5, 10, 15, ...), verify the outcome claims of the prior five entries against the actual state of the files cited. The first verification window runs at Entry 5, covering Entries 1–5. The next runs at Entry 10, covering Entries 6–10. And so on.

**What to verify.** For each entry's outcome field, identify the discrete claims (e.g., "principle #X added to file Y," "section Z added to file W," "new file V created"). For each claim:
- Does the cited content exist where the claim says?
- Does the cited file exist if it was claimed to be created?
- Are filename references correct?
- Are cross-references that would need updating actually updated?

**What happens when verification finds residuals.** Per the chain Append protocol, prior entries are not edited. Residuals are fixed in a new entry that explicitly captures both the correction and the original entry's overclaim. The new entry's outcome explicitly notes which prior entry's claim was the source of the residual; this preserves the historical record while updating going-forward state. The pattern matches the timestamp-correction pattern from Entry 4 — capture the drift, fix forward, preserve the originals.

**What happens when verification finds nothing.** No new entry needed; verification can be noted as a Change Log row in `cohort_temporal_record.md` (e.g., "Verification pass over Entries N through N+4: all outcome claims confirmed").

**Implementation.** Verification can be partially automated (grep against cited file paths and content patterns) and partially manual (judgment about whether a section's content actually delivers what the entry's outcome claims). The `verify_chain.py` script handles hash integrity; an analogous `verify_outcomes.py` could handle outcome verification. Building it is deferred until the cadence is exercised enough to know what patterns are worth automating.

**Retroactive verification of prior windows.** When a verification pass re-examines a prior window (because the framework gains a new window-completion mechanism, because an audit triggers a re-pass, or because a stakeholder requests one), the re-examination requires a fresh grep against current file state. A previous-session verification report that pronounced a window clean is itself an unverified data point; treating it as ground truth substitutes claim-to-have-already-checked for actual verification. The compound-overclaim pattern surfaced during the 2026-05-07 evening session demonstrates the failure mode: an Entry-7 claim that Window 1 (Entries 1–5) had been retroactively verified clean was carried forward in subsequent work as if Window 1 were settled, when in fact several Window-1 outcome claims still had residuals. Fresh-grep requirement closes this gap. The intake-skepticism principle (operating constraint #6) applies here too: the agent's own prior verification claims are intake that requires skepticism, not testimony that bypasses it. *Forward-ported as Entry 14 from orphan-Entry 11 on the divergent 20260507_evening track; original orphan-chain context: SHA-256 chain head `03e76a5b92c000f5d5ef412954d41baf2bf573fcab7770d005b109357205ddf2` (orphan-Entry 11).*

**Composition-time discipline.** When composing a chain entry's outcome field, the agent runs grep against current file state for each discrete claim before writing the outcome (per operating constraint #9 in `core_principles_for_agent_behavior.md` — this subsection is the records-side mirror). Composition-time grep is the *first* verification layer; cadence (above) is the second; retroactive verification of prior windows (above) is the third. The three layers are complementary: composition-time prevents the failure mode at its source, cadence catches anything composition-time missed within a bounded window, retroactive catches drift between cadence windows. The compound-overclaim pattern surfaced repeatedly across Entries 1, 5, 9, 12 demonstrated that the protocol's prior two-layer architecture left a gap at the composition level — entries authored from intent rather than from grep evidence consistently overclaimed on small-file edits while substantive work landed correctly. Composition-time discipline closes that gap structurally. *Forward-ported as Entry 15 from orphan-Entry 12 on the divergent 20260507_evening track; original orphan-chain context: SHA-256 chain head `4db79acebb556138f426cbbc2ee3b5f967606ec7aa01b22a7b0cdf8bddfa5324` (orphan-Entry 12).*

**Cadence patterns at multiple scales.** Chain-entry verification cadence and major-patch handoff cadence are coupled, with both operating on accumulated effort rather than entry count. Each entry self-tags an effort estimate at composition time on a 1/3/5 scale (Small / Medium / Large) — small enough that estimation is fast, large enough that the tags differentiate. Composition-time effort tagging doubles as a sanity check against the small-inline-edits-overclaim pattern: if an entry's intent suggests M but the outcome covers more than three discrete file changes, the L tag is more honest. Major-patch handoff threshold is 15 accumulated effort units since the last major-patch handoff (calibrated empirically over the first few cycles). Hybrid ceiling: every 10 entries triggers a major-patch handoff regardless of accumulated effort, to prevent indefinite deferral if entry composition keeps tagging S. The handoff cadence supersedes the count-based "every-fifth-entry" framing in the original Cadence subsection above; the count-based framing is preserved as historical record per the protocol's append-only discipline. *Forward-ported as Entry 15 from orphan-Entry 12 (count-based version) supplemented by orphan-Entry 13's revision to effort-based; original orphan-chain context: orphan-Entry 12 SHA-256 chain head `4db79acebb556138f426cbbc2ee3b5f967606ec7aa01b22a7b0cdf8bddfa5324`; orphan-Entry 13 SHA-256 chain head `d9beaf977411c133f36e1adf6fd2c9b1f0cbbbb8eafef375c05e551ecbc38c37`. Per session decision D3, this entry adopts effort-based cadence directly without instantiating the count-based interim.*

**Claim-versus-record divergence detection.** When a stakeholder claim arrives that the records cover (about a person, event, framework detail, or historical fact), the agent runs an observe-and-compare check between the claim and current record state before integrating the claim. Per operating constraint #10 in `core_principles_for_agent_behavior.md`, the agent surfaces divergence explicitly with the integration paths named (records stale or wrong, claim wrong, both partially right, claim additive). This subsection is the records-side mirror of constraint #10 and constitutes the *fourth* verification layer alongside composition-time (1st, this entry above), cadence (2nd, "Cadence" subsection above), and retroactive (3rd, "Retroactive verification of prior windows" subsection above). The four layers together form the protocol's complete coverage: composition-time prevents source-level overclaim; cadence catches anything composition-time missed within a bounded window; retroactive catches drift between cadence windows; claim-versus-record divergence catches mismatch at intake time when new claims would otherwise overwrite or silently disagree with existing records. The trust-erosion-primacy framing from the 2026-05-07 evening course-corrections capture (`course_corrections_data_and_replication_20260507.md`) anchors the rationale: silently smoothing claim-record divergence is the most trust-erosion-dangerous mode because it makes the smoothing invisible to subsequent verification — only explicit divergence-surfacing makes the records auditable against intake. *Forward-ported as Entry 16 from orphan-Entry 13 on the divergent 20260507_evening track; original orphan-chain context: SHA-256 chain head `d9beaf977411c133f36e1adf6fd2c9b1f0cbbbb8eafef375c05e551ecbc38c37` (orphan-Entry 13).*

**Post-commit verification routine.** Chain entries that produce canonical-state file changes ship with a `verify_entry_N.py` script the stakeholder (or next session) runs after the file changes are applied to project knowledge. Pattern established by `verify_entry_17.py`. The routine verifies (a) presence of all anchor strings the entry's outcome claims (substring or regex matches against cited file paths), and (b) chain integrity by parsing the entry's AAR fields from the canonical file, recomputing the SHA-256 hash from the field concatenation, and comparing to the recorded `this_entry_hash`. PASS/FAIL output is the canonical post-commit signal. This is the *fifth* verification layer alongside composition-time (1st), cadence (2nd), retroactive (3rd), and claim-versus-record divergence detection (4th). The structural gap this layer closes is between agent composition-time verification (operates against working state — the agent's intra-session file modifications) and post-commit canonical state (what landed in project knowledge after the stakeholder applies the file replacements). This is a double-loop response (per the Argyris/Schön frame) to the recurring multi-file-outcome overclaim pattern (Entries 1, 5, 12, 14, 16): composition-time discipline alone is necessary but insufficient for chain integrity, and the recurring pattern after composition-time strengthening is signal that the gap requires a new layer rather than another tightening of the existing layers. The discipline is part of the cross-session handshake protocol documented in `README.md`. *Codified as Entry 18; pattern instantiated by `verify_entry_17.py` per Entry 17 outcome.* Hardened in Entry 19 with the **hash-manifest discipline**: each chain entry that produces canonical-state file changes captures the SHA-256 of each produced file at composition time and embeds the hashes as an `EXPECTED_HASHES` dict at the top of `verify_entry_N.py`; the handoff document mirrors the same manifest in human-readable form. The hash check runs first (definitive byte-equality against expected manifest) and content-pattern checks remain as the diagnostic layer surfaced by hash mismatch. Hash check closes a composition-time-drift mode that content-pattern verification alone cannot catch — where the verify script and handoff both reflect the same composition-time understanding and can be wrong in the same direction; mechanical linkage via composition-time hashing means all three artifacts (produced files, handoff manifest, verify EXPECTED_HASHES) derive from one fingerprint and any divergence is mechanical. The hash-manifest discipline parallels the hash-chain integrity discipline in use for entries since genesis: both use hash-mechanical linkage to close drift between authoritative-at-time content and downstream artifacts. Hash check is necessary but not sufficient — content-pattern checks catch outcome-correctness mismatches that hashes don't surface (e.g. Claude composes a wrong file consistently across all three artifacts). Verify routine ordering is hash-first (definitive), content-patterns-second (diagnostic), chain-integrity-third (authentication). *Codified as Entry 19; first canonical instance is `verify_entry_19.py`; motivated by seventh-instance recurrence of the multi-file-outcome overclaim pattern on `cohort_temporal_record.md` across Entries 17, 18, and 19.* Hardened further in Entry 20 with the **modification-confirmation discipline**: each chain entry that produces canonical-state file changes also captures the SHA-256 of each modified file *as observed in canonical state before modification* and embeds those hashes as a `PRIOR_HASHES` dict at the top of `verify_entry_N.py` alongside `EXPECTED_HASHES`. The verify routine runs a Layer 0 inequality check first (`PRIOR_HASHES[F] ≠ EXPECTED_HASHES[F]` for every claimed-modified `F`) before any other layer. This closes the composition-layer no-write fabrication mode that the hash-manifest discipline alone cannot catch — hash-manifest catches "produced file didn't land in canonical state" (transmission-layer drift); modification-confirmation catches "claim of modification with no actual delta produced" (composition-layer no-write). The two disciplines together form complete coverage of the modification-claim verification space: composition-time (Layer 0: PRIOR ≠ EXPECTED) catches no-write fabrication; post-commit hash-manifest (Layer 1: produced equals EXPECTED) catches transmission drift; content-pattern checks (Layer 2) remain as diagnostic surface; chain-integrity check (Layer 3) authenticates the entry. The taxonomy refinement is significant: Entries 17 and 18 actually exhibited composition-layer no-write on `cohort_temporal_record.md` (claims of modification without produced delta — the third-attempt closure in Entry 19 was effectively the first time the file was actually composed correctly), whereas Entry 19 exhibited transmission-layer drift on the same file (correctly produced but not landed in canonical). The modification-confirmation discipline retroactively explains why Entry 19's hash-manifest discipline was necessary-but-not-sufficient and what additional structural response closes the remaining gap. The discipline is the *third* instance of hash-mechanical linkage as the structural response when content-pattern verification has architectural limits (chain integrity since genesis; post-commit hash-manifest in Entry 19; modification-confirmation in Entry 20), suggesting hash-mechanical linkage is a recursively-applicable framework-level pattern. *Codified as Entry 20; first canonical instance is `verify_entry_20.py`; motivated by stakeholder structural review surfacing the directional gap in Entry 19's hash-manifest discipline, in the context of the eighth-instance recurrence of the multi-file-outcome overclaim pattern (fourth consecutive instance on `cohort_temporal_record.md` — Entry 19's produced file did not land canonically and Entry 20 is the fourth-attempt closure of canonical landing).* Hardened further still in Entry 23 with the **rename-as-navigation discipline** as the response when the verification layers persistently detect a transit failure on a specific file across multiple entries despite correct production. The discipline is cause-agnostic by design: rename the file with `_modN` suffix and update read-instruction pointers; whatever caching/divergence layer keys on filename treats the new path as a different object regardless of what mechanism is actually responsible. Distinct from the prior verification disciplines (which detect) and from the diagnostic instrumentation pattern of Entries 21–22 (which probes mechanism), rename-as-navigation is the navigation move available *without* mechanism understanding. It complements the diagnose-before-codify principle: diagnose-before-codify protects against codifying mechanism-claiming responses prematurely; rename-as-navigation is what remains operationally available when mechanism is not yet known and operational unblocking takes precedence over diagnostic completeness. Worked example: `cohort_temporal_record.md` exhibited seven consecutive non-landings across Entries 17–22 (composition-layer no-write, transmission-layer drift, two diagnostic-marker iterations); Entry 23 renames it to `cohort_temporal_record_mod1.md`, satisfying the diagnostic marker's self-documenting lifecycle by reframing canonical landing as the new filename's first landing rather than the old filename's belated landing. Codified as Entry 23; first canonical instance is the rename of cohort_temporal_record.md to cohort_temporal_record_mod1.md; further `_mod2`, `_mod3`, … increments available if mod-1 also fails to land. The discipline is documented in `README.md` Cross-session handshake protocol section as component #7. Hardened further still in Entry 24 with the **timestamped-spine + mod-N-lateral versioning + handoff-as-state-pointer discipline** as the structural response to the tapeworm-recursion mechanism finding (mass-upload static-name caching where some [0, n] of static-named files in a mass upload retain their cached prior version rather than the freshly-dropped version; codified as glossary entry "Tapeworm recursion"; mechanism remains undiagnosed and the codification is mechanism-agnostic per principle #22 scope-clarification). The discipline is paired by artifact role: the chain entry file (this file's role) is versioned along its temporal axis as `framework_decisions_record_<TIMESTAMP>.md` paired with the existing `handoff_<TIMESTAMP>.md` naming convention, with the (handoff_T, spine_T) pair becoming the canonical durable unit for cross-temporal state recovery; lateral reference files (`framework_principles.md`, `project_rigger_glossary.md`, `core_principles_for_agent_behavior.md`, plus `cohort_temporal_record_mod1.md` already at mod1 from Entry 23) are versioned with `_modN` integer suffixes incremented on each modification; README stays static-named as the entry point with verify-then-redrop as its mitigation; the handoff document gains a lateral-file mod manifest making (handoff_T, spine_T) strictly deterministic for full state recovery rather than near-deterministic. The discipline is mechanism-agnostic by design: timestamping the spine and mod-N'ing the laterals both produce fresh filenames that bypass whatever the batch-upload cache is keyed on, regardless of what mechanism that turns out to be. Distinct from rename-as-navigation (Entry 23) by being preemptive structural discipline rather than reactive navigation pivot — rename-as-navigation fires after observed non-landing on a specific file; the Entry 24 discipline applies preemptively to a specified file set so non-landing is structurally bypassed for those files. Lazy instantiation is part of the discipline: files in the candidate set are mod1'd on first modification post-discipline-adoption, not bulk-renamed at the discipline's introduction; this avoids creating mod versions that would be byte-identical to static-named files that would just become orphans. The recursive (handoff_T, spine_T) pairing extends the chain-hash integrity property (each entry references predecessor by hash) across the directional axis as well as the state axis: handoff_T's chain-head pointer indexes into spine_T's last entry; spine_T's last entry's intent and why fields reference what handoff_(T-1) directed. This is the load-bearing structural insight that makes the framework substrate-grade for cross-temporal handoff — anyone reading at arbitrary future T gets both what we thought we were doing and what we actually recorded in paired form, with each artifact anchoring the other. *Codified as Entry 24; first canonical instances are `framework_decisions_record_20260510T010156Z.md` (the spine for this entry), `cohort_temporal_record_mod2.md` (advancing the existing mod1), and `project_rigger_glossary_mod1.md` (first lateral mod1 instantiation under the discipline); motivated by three-instance recurrence of the tapeworm-recursion mechanism (Entries 22, 23, 24 openers) with the third instance on a previously-unfragile file (project_rigger_glossary.md) falsifying the file-specific filename-keyed hypothesis from Entry 23 open work item #1.* The discipline is documented in `README.md` Cross-session handshake protocol section as components #8 (Timestamped spine) and #9 (Mod-N versioning). Hardened further still in Entry 31 with the **apply-checklist completeness discipline** as the structural response to the composition-time author-side blindness to stakeholder-executable file-system operations that Entry 30's archive narrative surfaced. The discipline extends operating constraint #9 in `core_principles_for_agent_behavior_mod1.md` (the grep-evidence-based outcome composition constraint) to cover **all** file-system operations the entry's intent implies, not only those Claude can execute directly. Mechanism: chain entries enumerate REMOVE operations alongside NEW/REPLACE operations in the outcome field; the paired handoff's apply-checklist mirrors every operation as a discrete row the stakeholder can execute through the project-knowledge interface; the verify script's manifest supports each operation type with `EXPECTED_HASHES` (Layer 1 hash-manifest check for files-that-should-exist) and a new `ABSENT_FILES` set (Layer 1.5 absence check for files-that-should-not-exist, sitting between Layer 1 hash-manifest and Layer 2 content-pattern). This is the *fourth* instance of hash-mechanical and filesystem-mechanical linkage as the structural response when content-pattern verification has architectural limits (chain integrity since genesis; post-commit hash-manifest in Entry 19; modification-confirmation in Entry 20; absence-check in Entry 31), further confirming hash-mechanical and filesystem-mechanical linkage as a recursively-applicable framework-level pattern. The taxonomy refinement is significant: Layer 1 catches "produced file didn't land" (presence/hash mismatch on a claimed-existing file); Layer 1.5 catches "removed file didn't get removed" (presence on a claimed-absent file); together Layers 1 and 1.5 cover both directions of the file-system-state claim space. The discipline is hierarchically placed at operating-principle tier (constraint #9 in `core_principles_for_agent_behavior_mod1.md`, reading-order item #18) rather than glossary-lookup tier alone, reflecting the meta-insight that disciplines belong where the visibility requirement places them in the principle hierarchy — the operating-memory-vs-glossary-lookup distinction is structural rather than cosmetic. *Codified as Entry 31; first canonical instance is `verify_entry_31.py`; motivated by the Entry 30 archive operations narrating "23 files migrated to GitHub-hosted archive tier" / "removed from canonical project-knowledge surface" without enumerating the 23 stakeholder-side REMOVE operations in the apply-checklist, with ground-truth at Entry 31 thread-open confirming all 23 files remained inline (corpus at 95 files / 5.1 MB vs the expected ~67 files / ~2.25 MB).* The `Apply-checklist completeness` glossary entry in `project_rigger_glossary_mod7.md` is the terminology pointer back to this discipline; the operating constraint surface in `core_principles_for_agent_behavior_mod1.md` is the authoritative codification.

This protocol is itself append-only; future verification windows may surface protocol gaps that warrant updates here.
